## Bugs found (Phase 1 QA — by tester, coder must fix)

1. **[Backend bug, confirmed by test] Potion healing doesn't work at all.**
   `backend/routes/scene.js:84` — the `/iksir/i.test(item.name)` regex doesn't match the Turkish dotted capital **İ** (U+0130) in item names. When `item.name` = `"İksir (Küçük İyileştirme)"` this regex returns `false` (JS's Unicode case-folding doesn't apply Turkish locale rules — even `"İ".toLowerCase()` doesn't produce `"i"` but `"i̇"` — an i plus a combining dot). Result: using a potion via `POST /api/scene/item/use` removes the item from inventory, but **HP never increases**. Evidence: the "using a potion heals HP up to the maximum..." test in `backend/tests/scene.test.js` was deliberately left red (`hp.current` expects 6, gets 1).
   Suggested fix: instead of the regex, e.g. `item.name.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes('iksir')`, or add a locale-independent field like `type: 'potion'` to the item.

2. **[Frontend — major scope gap] Inventory actions (use/equip/drop/throw) aren't in the UI at all.**
   On the backend side, `POST /api/scene/item/use|equip|drop|throw` fully works (confirmed by tests) and `frontend/src/api.ts` even has `useItem`/`equipItem`/`dropItem` wrappers written — but no component calls them, and there's no `throwItem` function at all. `CharacterCard.tsx` shows the inventory only as a static list, with no clickable actions. `PROJECT.md` explicitly requests "inventory (use/equip/drop/throw)" as part of the Phase 1 scope — as it stands, the scope is incomplete.

3. **[Frontend i18n bug, confirmed by test] The character card shows the raw id instead of the Turkish race/class name.**
   `CharacterCard.tsx:21` writes `{character.race} · {character.class}` — these are the raw ids coming from the backend (`"human"`, `"fighter"`), only capitalized via CSS `text-transform: capitalize` ("Human · Fighter"), but the rest of the UI is entirely in Turkish (İnsan, Savaşçı, Elf, Büyücü...). Evidence: the last test in `frontend/src/components/CharacterCard.test.tsx` is deliberately red.
   Suggested fix: look up the `name` field from the races/classes list returned by `getCharacterOptions()` and display that (the App already loads races/classes state globally — it can be passed to CharacterCard as a prop, or the id→name map moved into context).

4. **[Frontend bug] Refreshing the page (F5) goes back to the character creation screen, the current character is lost.**
   `App.tsx` keeps character state only in a React `useState`, it doesn't restore the active character from the backend via `getCurrentCharacter()` (already present in api.ts, never called). While the character still exists on the backend (a single global "active character" slot), if the user refreshes the page they see the creation form again; if they fill it out and submit again, **the old character on the backend gets silently overwritten by the new one** (HP/inventory state is lost).

5. **[Behavior / needs clarification — may not be a bug] Clicking the grid during the enemy's turn actually moves the enemy's token.**
   There's no AI/automatic enemy movement in Phase 1. `handleCellClick` in `TacticalGrid.tsx` always targets `scene.activeTokenId` — after pressing "End Turn" and the turn passing to the Goblin, if the user clicks any cell they effectively **move the Goblin themselves** (confirmed via curl, no console error, works silently). There's nothing in the UI preventing or explaining this. Needs clarification with PM/coder on whether this is a deliberate placeholder until AI is added in Phase 2, or should be blocked now.

6. **[Minor / code smell] The 404 "Token not found" branch in `routes/scene.js`'s POST /move is effectively dead code.**
   Since the `scene.activeTokenId !== tokenId` check already runs first, sending a nonexistent `tokenId` (as long as it doesn't match the active token id) always returns 400 first; the 404 can only be reached if `activeTokenId` itself isn't in `scene.tokens`, which doesn't happen in normal flow. Not a functional bug, just noticed while writing tests.

7. **[Test infrastructure note — doesn't affect the coder but future test writers should know] `backend/data/store.js` singletons can duplicate under a CJS/ESM mix.**
   The backend uses `require`/`module.exports` (CommonJS). If a test file pulls the same store via an ESM `import` (since Vitest's `vite-node` transform can diverge from the native `require()` cache), even if the test calls `characters.clear()`, the REAL Map used by the routes doesn't get cleared — tests appear to fail randomly. Fix: always pull the app code via `require()` using `createRequire(import.meta.url)` in `backend/tests/*.test.js` (already done in the existing 3 test files). Anyone writing a new backend test file should follow the same pattern.

## Phase 2 — Real AI Game Master (Google Gemini API)

User decision: **Gemini API** (free tier) instead of the Claude API — due to cost/bottleneck concerns. The user will provide a Gemini API key shortly (aistudio.google.com/apikey, available for free).

Design principles (PM):
- AI is NOT the game's backbone — it's an "overlay layer." If there's no key / it errors / the rate limit is exceeded, the system **automatically and silently** falls back to the existing mock GM (Phase 1 flavor text). The game must never crash from depending on AI.
- In Phase 2, AI only generates **narration** (a chat message). Game state (HP, inventory, token position) continues to be managed by the existing rule-based backend, not the AI — having the AI mutate state is risky/out of scope, to be considered later.
- Rate limit: a reasonable per-session/hourly cap (e.g. 30 AI calls per hour) — also protects the free tier quota.
- `GEMINI_API_KEY` is read from an environment variable, **never committed** (`.env` must be in `.gitignore`). Until the key arrives, the system keeps working by automatically falling back to the mock.

### Coder
- [x] `backend`: `.env.example` added (`GEMINI_API_KEY=`, `PORT=`), `.env` added to `.gitignore`, loaded in `server.js` via `dotenv`
- [x] `backend`: `@google/generative-ai` added, model `gemini-3.6-flash` (overridable via `GEMINI_MODEL` env)
- [x] `backend`: `services/aiGm.js` — builds a prompt from character + scene + last 6 messages and returns narration text from Gemini
- [x] `backend`: `services/rateLimiter.js` — hourly fixed-window counter, default limit 30 (overridable via `AI_HOURLY_LIMIT`)
- [x] `backend`: `routes/chat.js` wired to the AI layer — no key/rate limit exceeded/Gemini error → silently falls back to the `gmFlavor.js` mock, `gmMessage.source` field (`"ai"`/`"mock"`) added
- [x] Verified without a key (via curl: returns `source: "mock"`) and with an invalid key (Gemini 400 error logged, silently falls back to mock).
- [x] **Verified with a real valid key.** The first few keys returned a `429 "prepayment credits depleted"` error in different Gemini projects (a user-side billing issue, required no code change — only the default model name was updated from `gemini-2.5-flash` → `gemini-3.6-flash`, commit 07e977e). Successful with the user's final key from a different Google Cloud project: `source: "ai"`, Turkish/atmospheric/context-appropriate narration came through, prior context was used correctly across a multi-turn chat. Also, one real-traffic request got a transient `503 "high demand"` from Gemini and the system silently fell back to mock and returned to AI on the next request — both the success and the transient-error scenario were verified live.

Note: `services/sceneState.js` was added — `scene.js`'s dedicated `getScene()` was moved into a shared module between `routes/scene.js` and `routes/chat.js` since the AI prompt also needs the scene context (a small refactor, behavior unchanged).

### Tester
- [x] By mocking the Gemini call: tests written for (a) successful AI response scenario, (b) error/timeout → mock fallback scenario, (c) rate limit exceeded → mock fallback scenario — `backend/tests/aiGmFallback.test.js` (5 tests, doesn't require a real API key)
- [x] Test the rate limiter's counter logic — `backend/tests/rateLimiter.test.js` (5 tests: accepted within limit, rejected over limit, default limit, resets when the window fills, doesn't reset before the window fills)
- [x] A real manual QA pass after the key was added — **complete** (see below)
- [x] Phase 2 items added to QA_CHECKLIST.md

**Test infrastructure note (continuation of Bug #7):** `vi.mock()` doesn't work in this backend (CommonJS) — Vitest's ESM module graph can't intercept plain `.js` CommonJS files' own internal `require()` calls. In `aiGmFallback.test.js`, fake `aiGm`/`rateLimiter` modules were instead injected into `require.cache` BEFORE chat.js is loaded (native Node require-cache technique). If a similar service needs mocking in the future, follow the comment + pattern in this file, don't try `vi.mock`.

Test status: backend **57/57**, frontend **12/12** green.

**Full manual QA pass with real AI (2026-08-22, tester) — COMPLETE:** With the user's new key (different Google Cloud project):
- A real 3-turn chat (with an Elf/Wizard character, dungeon/chest scenario) — all `source: "ai"`, Turkish/atmospheric, containing race/class-specific details ("your sharp elven eyes", "your staff's shadow"), and turns referenced each other consistently (the AI is genuinely using the `recentMessages` context).
- State mutation check: character (HP/mana/inventory) and scene (token position/loot/round) compared before/after chat, field by field — **no field changed**, the AI only produces narration.
- Real rate-limit overflow: backend started with `AI_HOURLY_LIMIT=2` and 4 messages sent → 1-2 `source:"ai"`, 3-4 `source:"mock"` (exactly as expected), the "(mock)" label appeared correctly in the browser.
- The earlier billing-blocker (429) scenario had also been separately verified live by both the coder and the tester (fallback worked flawlessly) — see the Phase 2 section of QA_CHECKLIST.md.

**Phase 2 CLOSED.** No known open items.

**Environment note (tester, discovered in this session):** The `lsof` command does NOT exist in this Windows/Git-Bash environment — the `lsof -ti:PORT | xargs kill` pattern silently becomes a no-op and a stale node process can keep running in the background, which led to confusing results like "I restarted but the state is still old." Correct method: find the PID with `netstat -ano | grep ":PORT" | grep LISTENING` and use `taskkill //PID <pid> //F`.

## Phase 3 — Character Creation, BG3-Inspired Combat System, Richer AI Narration

Feedback the user gave after testing by hand (2026-08-22). Scope is large, user decision: given as a single Phase 3, the team should implement it in a sensible order (suggested order A→E below, but not a strict rule). The current screen layout (adventure log/chat centered, tactical grid on the side, inventory) was APPROVED by the user, WILL NOT CHANGE.

### A) Character creation flow — redesign [x] COMPLETE (commit ba79a89)
- [x] Stat assignment is now determined **randomly by rolling a D20, not manual selection** — `POST /api/character/roll-stats` (with race bonus applied), a short "rolling dice" animation on the frontend (random values ~700ms) settles on the real result
- [x] New step: **appearance** selection — 5 options in `data/appearances.js`, dropdown added to the form
- [x] After name entry, before moving to the game screen, an **AI-generated opening story** is shown — `POST /api/character/intro`, a separate prompt (`generateOpeningStory`), falls back to the `data/openingFlavor.js` mock template if there's no key/an error occurs (consistent with the Phase 2 principle), the new `IntroScreen.tsx` moves to the game with "Continue". The opening message is also added to the chat history as the first GM message.

**Known test breakages (intentional design change, tester should update):**
- `backend/tests/character.test.js`: the "human race applies +1 bonus to all attributes" test expected a deterministic 11/11/..., now the roll is random — the RNG needs to be mocked (the require-cache pattern from Bug #7 can also be used for `services/dice.js`).
- Frontend: `CharacterCreation.test.tsx`, `App.test.tsx`, `CharacterCard.test.tsx` assume the old single-step flow / the old `Character` type (no appearance field) — `tsc -b` (i.e. `npm run build`) gives type errors in these files. Application code (excluding tests) is clean with both `tsc --noEmit` and `vite build`.

### B) Screen layout — NO CHANGE
- Adventure log (chat) centered, tactical grid on the side, inventory stays as-is — approved by the user.

### C) Tactical/combat system — inspired by Baldur's Gate 3 [x] COMPLETE (commit 02751c1)
- [x] Movement allowance: max per turn increased from **4 → 5** cells (`data/sceneFactory.js`)
- [x] **Action economy**: `actionAvailable`/`bonusActionAvailable` added to tokens, `end-turn` resets them for the new active token. PM-approved scope: using + throwing an item spends an Action (and returns 400 if already spent/not the player's turn), equip/unequip/drop remain free. Bonus Action is not currently consumed by any action (no action in the current scope needs it, the field is ready and waiting).
- [x] **Spell/mana system**: PM-approved decision — a separate 5e slot table was not added, the existing mana pool continues to serve as the spell resource (since there's no concrete spell list yet).

### D) AI narration quality [x] COMPLETE (commit 02751c1)
- [x] Instruction to describe **5 senses** added to the prompt (`services/aiGm.js` buildPrompt)
- [x] **Dice-based action resolution**: `services/actionResolver.js` — the relevant stat is inferred from the message (attack→STRENGTH, inspection→WISDOM, persuasion→CHARISMA, other→DEXTERITY), success/failure/critical is determined via D20+modifier vs DC 12. The roll happens on the backend, is given to the AI as context, and the AI narrates based ONLY on the outcome (doesn't make up its own roll) — consistent with the Phase 2 principle. The mock fallback also adds a short extra sentence based on the outcome. The `gmMessage.roll` field returns full roll detail for QA/debug, shown in ChatPanel.

**Known minor gap:** Action detection uses a simple keyword regex (similar to the category logic in gmFlavor.js) — some spelling variants (e.g. dotless/dotted i in Turkish) for words like "saldırıyorum" (I attack) can fall through to the default "DEXTERITY" instead of the expected "STRENGTH". Not a functional bug (the dice mechanic works correctly, only which stat is used is sometimes a guess), but noting it.

### E) Improve throwing UX [x] COMPLETE (commit 59727ef)
- [x] The x/y form was removed; clicking "Throw" now enters grid target-selection mode (BG3 style), throwing happens by clicking a cell.

### Tester
- [x] Tests were updated/expanded as A, E, C, D were implemented — backend **104/104** (new: `dice.test.js`, `characterIntro.test.js`, `actionResolver.test.js`, Action-economy gating tests added to `scene.test.js`, a `roll` field test added to `chat.test.js`), frontend **31/31** (new: `ChatPanel.test.tsx` rewritten from scratch, throw-mode + Action indicator tests added to `TacticalGrid.test.tsx`, `CharacterCreation.test.tsx` rewritten for the D20 flow). `tsc -b` and `vite build` clean.
- [x] A full regression QA pass was done at the end of Phase 3 (end-to-end with Playwright/chromium) — see below and `QA_CHECKLIST.md`

**Phase 3 (A, C, D, E) fully closed** — coder implementation + tester test/QA pass done. B never needed any change.

**Follow-up item (PM, low priority, not a blocker):** In `actionResolver.js`, `detectActionAttribute`'s WISDOM keyword `ara` is an unanchored substring, so it's incorrectly triggered by words containing the very common Turkish syllable "-ara-" (`duvarA`, `kaçARAk`, `karanlığA`...) — verified by the tester with concrete examples in `actionResolver.test.js`, a broader root cause than the "ı/i variant" issue the coder had already noted. The dice mechanic isn't broken, it's just sometimes rolled with the wrong stat/modifier. Suggestion: a word-bounded regex like `\bara\b` instead of `ara`. Does not block the Phase 3 regression.

**New bug found (tester, regression QA — can be picked up in Phase 3.5, not a blocker):** The "Action: ✓/✗ · Bonus: ✓/✗" indicator in TacticalGrid does not update IMMEDIATELY after action-consuming actions (Use, Throw) triggered from inventory via `CharacterCard`. The backend correctly consumes the Action right (a second use is genuinely rejected with 400 "You have no Action left for this turn." — verified live), but the indicator incorrectly keeps showing "✓" until the next scene fetch (move/End Turn/grid-based throw). Root cause: `TacticalGrid` keeps its own `scene` state and only calls `setScene` for actions it triggers itself (`move`/`end-turn`/grid-throw); `CharacterCard`'s `onCharacterChange` callback doesn't notify `TacticalGrid`. (Throwing via the grid — a flow TacticalGrid manages itself — updates the indicator correctly, which confirms the root cause.) Suggestion: lift the scene state up into `App.tsx`, or trigger a scene refetch after `CharacterCard`'s action-consuming calls too.

**Minor content note:** The mock GM response + outcome suffix combination can sometimes clash in tone — example: "Your opponent staggers back." (ATTACK_RESPONSES template) + "And things went about as badly as they could have." (critical-failure suffix) ended up next to each other in the same sentence. Not functional, just a reading-experience note.

**QA note:** A real Gemini AI response could not be verified in this regression pass — the key used in the previous Phase 2 pass has run out of quota (429), so the intro + chat flows fell back to mock. The fallback again worked flawlessly (no console/page errors), but Phase 3's "5 senses description" and dice-outcome-contextual AI narration have not yet been visually verified with a real AI response — can be retried with a new/full-quota key.

## Phase 4 — Movement Bugs, Equipment Slots, Simple Enemy AI, BG3 Visual Style

Feedback the user gave after testing by hand. User decisions: enemy AI will be **simple scripted** behavior (move toward the player + attack if adjacent, no complex strategy, NO AI/LLM call — consistent with the cost/bottleneck principle); the "make it like BG3" request is scoped to **visual/aesthetic** only (color, button style, panel layout) — combat-mechanic depth (cover, elevation, etc.) and AI image generation are OUT OF SCOPE for this phase.

### Coder — in priority order (A and B are real bugs, those first) [x] COMPLETE
- [x] Bug A (commit 3652ea3): added Bresenham path checking to `/scene/move` (`services/sceneState.js`) — if the straight line from source to target intersects an obstacle, it's rejected with "The path is blocked by an obstacle." Not full pathfinding (simple is enough, consistent with the PM's note).
- [x] Bug B (commit 3652ea3): root cause — the distance check was against a fixed `token.speed`, multiple `/move` calls in the same turn weren't cumulatively bounded (not a regression, it was simply never enforced). Added `movementLeft` to tokens, each move decrements it, `end-turn` resets it to `speed` for the new token. Added a "Movement: remaining/max" indicator on the frontend.
- [x] Equipment slots (commit d54a62c): `data/itemSlots.js` — item name→slot mapping (head/chest/arms/legs/feet/hand, items without a slot map to null). `item/equip` now rejects an item whose slot is null (400), and unequips whatever's already in the same slot first if occupied. A 6-slot paper-doll view under an "Equipment" heading in CharacterCard.
- [x] Simple enemy AI (commit 91d9d64): `services/enemyAI.js` — when the enemy's turn comes up, it's resolved AUTOMATICALLY inside `end-turn` (greedy movement + D20+2 vs DC12 attack if adjacent, d6 damage on success), and the turn immediately returns to the player. No extra AI/LLM call, fully deterministic. Result messages are added to the chat history (`source: "mock"`).
- [x] BG3 visual style (commit d6c4f80): Google Fonts (Cinzel/Spectral), ornate panel frames with bronze/gold accents, gradient button/bar styles. Panel layout unchanged. Bonus: cleaned up leftover irrelevant Vite template content in `index.css`.

**Behavioral note (not a bug, by design):** `/scene/end-turn`'s response shape was preserved (still returns the scene flat/top-level, with `enemyMessages` added on top — backward compatible), but the BEHAVIOR changed: a single `end-turn` call now automatically resolves the enemy turn too if there is one and advances the turn all the way back to the player (previously two manual calls were required). The 3 old tests in `backend/tests/scene.test.js` assumed the old flow and needed updating.

### Tester
- [x] Regression tests for Bug A/B — `scene.test.js`: 400 when the target is empty but the path crosses an obstacle, works normally when the path is clear; cumulative distance across consecutive moves is rejected once it exceeds budget, accepted within budget, resets on end-turn

**End-to-end browser QA (Playwright, 2026-08-22):** Create character → game screen → equip item (appeared in the correct slot on the paper-doll, gold border) → try to move in one hop to an empty cell behind an obstacle (rejected: "The path is blocked by an obstacle.") → exceed the cumulative limit with two consecutive moves (rejected: "Target is out of range.", the "Movement: 2/5" indicator was correct) → End Turn (the enemy automatically approached, "The Goblin moves toward you." appeared in chat, the turn immediately returned to the player). No console/page errors (only the intentional test 400s and the initial 404).

**BG3 visual theme:** Cinzel/Spectral fonts load correctly (Google Fonts + fallback stack), ornate panels with bronze/gold accents, good contrast, no readability issues, no broken layout. Visually successful.

**⚠️ Finding — panel layout contradicts the approved spec:** Both Phase 3-B ("Adventure log (chat) centered, tactical grid on the side... approved by the user") and Phase 4-B ("left=character info, center=log/chat, right=tactical map") explicitly want the **chat in the CENTER**. But in the actual render (`App.css:31` — `grid-template-columns: 280px 1fr 320px`, DOM order `CharacterCard → TacticalGrid → ChatPanel`) the **tactical map is in the center (1fr, the widest column), chat is on the right** — the opposite of what's been approved twice. This isn't a new Phase 4 regression (the DOM/CSS order probably hasn't changed since Phase 1), but it was never fixed despite two separate user approvals. Confirmed with a screenshot. The fix is small: swapping the DOM order of `<TacticalGrid>` and `<ChatPanel>` in `App.tsx` may be enough (grid-template-columns' middle column is already `1fr` wide, better suited to chat).

**[x] Fixed (commit 673f7f0, coder):** swapped the `ChatPanel`/`TacticalGrid` DOM order in `App.tsx` (the middle column is now chat, the right column is the map). Right column width changed 320px→380px in `App.css` (so the 10-wide tactical grid fits more comfortably). tsc+build clean, frontend 38/38 green.

**[x] Visually verified by tester (2026-08-22):** DOM order checked in browser (Playwright) — `.app-main`'s children are now in the order `character-card → chat-panel → tactical-grid`, and the screenshot confirms left=character, center=Adventure Log (wide column), right=Abandoned Dungeon (tactical map, 380px) rendering correctly. No console errors. **The layout approved in Phase 3-B/4-B is now actually in effect.**

**Phase 4 FULLY closed — no known open items** (aside from the mock+outcome tone note and earlier low-priority notes, none of which are blockers).

## Phase 5 — Melee Mechanic, Auto-Narration After Movement, Icon-Based Inventory

Feedback the user gave with a hand-drawn diagram + screenshot (2026-08-22). Three items:

### Coder
- [x] **Melee/attack mechanic** (commit 9ef818f): `POST /api/scene/attack` — clicking an adjacent enemy token (no separate button in TacticalGrid, BG3-style click-to-attack) rolls D20 + the character class's primary attribute modifier vs DC 12 for hit, plus damage (d6, +d6 on a crit), consumes the Action, and the result is reflected both in the target's HP (removed from the scene at 0) and in chat (with AI/mock narration). Symmetric with `enemyAI.js`'s enemy-attack logic (same DC/damage die).
- [x] **Auto-narration after movement** (commit 9ef818f): when `/scene/move` succeeds, a short AI/mock narration is automatically generated and added to chat. The fallback principle is preserved. Note: the try-AI→fall-back-to-mock logic was extracted into `services/narrationService.js` (shared by chat.js, attack, and move — avoids code duplication).
- [x] **Icon-based inventory/equipment** (commit 3550ebe): `backend/scripts/extractIcons.js` — parses DMI metadata (the zTXt Description chunk) and cuts the correct state for each slot (not just the first tile — in some files the first state is deliberately an empty/animated placeholder, e.g. `head/default.dmi`, which I noticed visually as it rendered as "ERROR" text, then fixed the script to target the correct state, e.g. `head/hats.dmi` → "tophat") and wrote them as 12 PNGs under `frontend/public/icons/` — verified every one visually one by one with the Read tool (glasses, surgical mask, belt, gloves, hat, armor all clearly recognizable). Switched to SS13-style 13 slots (`data/itemSlots.js`): head/mask/glasses/ears/neck/back/suit/under/gloves/belt/shoes/accessories/hand. Existing items were remapped (Leather Armor→suit, Shield→back [SS13's list has no shield slot, closest match — noted], weapons→hand [no weapon icon in the asset set, emoji fallback ⚔]). The paper-doll is now 3 columns, iconed, and clickable (clicking a filled slot unequips it); every item in the inventory list shows a small icon. A CC BY-SA 3.0 attribution note was added to the App.tsx footer (license obligation).
  **Note:** since there's no visual preview tool for graphics/asset processing in this environment, I checked every icon one by one using the Read tool's visual rendering feature — this is how I caught that the initial approach (blindly taking the first tile) picked the wrong (empty/error placeholder) frame for `head`/`suit`; the PM's note that "the first state is usually fine" didn't hold for these two files.

## Phase 6 — Multi-User Infrastructure + Persistence + Game Loop

PM's own assessment (approved by the user): The project has been operating like a single-user demo so far — the backend has ONE global character/scene/chat state, everyone shares the same state, and everything lives in RAM (everything is wiped on a server restart). Moving forward without addressing this makes no sense for the "open up to users" goal. User decision: A+B (infrastructure) first, then C (game loop).

### A) Oturum izolasyonu (öncelik 1, en kritik) [x] TAMAMLANDI (commit 91e4ebf)
- [x] Frontend: `session.ts` — ilk ziyarette `crypto.randomUUID()` ile session ID üretilip `localStorage`'a (`dnd-session-id`) kaydediliyor, bellek-içi cache'leniyor; `api.ts`'teki her istek `X-Session-Id` header'ı gönderiyor
- [x] Backend: `services/sessionId.js` header'ı okuyor (yoksa `"default"`'e düşüyor — geriye dönük uyumluluk). `data/store.js`'e `activeCharacterIdBySession` eklendi; `chatHistories`/`scenes` zaten sessionId'ye göre anahtarlanan Map'lerdi, artık gerçek sessionId ile anahtarlanıyor (sabit `"default"` yerine). `characters` hâlâ characterId'ye göre global (id'ler benzersiz)
- [x] Rate limiter kasıtlı olarak GLOBAL bırakıldı (PM kararıyla tutarlı) — testle doğrulandı (`allowRequest()` parametresiz)
- [x] Var olmayan/eskimiş sessionId → mevcut karakter/sahne yok davranışıyla tutarlı (404 karakter yoksa, sahne lazy-init ediliyor)

**Tester notu (mimari, bug değil):** `item/use|equip|drop|throw` ve `/attack` endpoint'leri `characterId`'yi body'den alıp doğrudan global `characters` Map'inde arıyor — sessionId'nin o characterId'nin GERÇEK sahibi olduğunu doğrulamıyor. nanoid'ler tahmin edilemez olduğu için pratik risk düşük, ama mimari olarak "characterId bilmek = erişim" varsayımına dayanıyor. Test edilip belgelendi (`sessionIsolation.test.js`), bug olarak açılmadı — ileride (özellikle B/kalıcılık sonrası, hesaplar kalıcı hale gelince) tekrar gözden geçirilmesi önerilir.

### B) Kalıcılık (öncelik 2) [x] TAMAMLANDI (commit 170adf3)
- [x] `backend/data/db.js` — `better-sqlite3` ile tek dosyalık DB (`game.db`), 4 tablo: characters/scenes/chat_histories/sessions. - [x] `backend/services/persistence.js` — repository katmanı: `loadAll()` sunucu açılışında DB'deki her şeyi in-memory Map'lere yüklüyor, `saveCharacter/saveScene/saveChatHistory/saveActiveCharacterId` her mutasyon noktasından çağrılıyor. **Tasarım kararı:** mevcut route kodu nesneleri hep REFERANSLA mutasyona uğratıyor (`.set()`'i tekrar çağırmadan) — "şeffaf DB-destekli Map" yaklaşımı bunu sessizce bozardı (her `get()` DB'den taze bir kopya dönerdi, mutasyonlar hiçbir yere yazılmazdı). Bunun yerine in-memory Map çalışma-zamanı otoritesi olarak kaldı, SQLite sadece restart'lar arası bir "gölge" kopya — her mutasyon noktasına (move/end-turn/attack/item aksiyonları/character create-update/chat/intro) açıkça `saveX()` çağrısı eklendi.

**Doğrulama:** izole bir `DB_PATH` ile karakter oluşturdum, hareket ettim, sohbet ettim, sunucuyu tamamen kapatıp yeniden başlattım — karakter/pozisyon/sohbet geçmişi birebir korundu. backend 175/175, frontend 57/57 yeşil, tsc+build temiz.

- [x] `App.test.tsx`'e Faz 6-C bloğu (3 test): `hp.current<=0` olunca `GameOverScreen` gösteriliyor (oyun ekranı değil), "Yeniden Başla" `resetSession()`'ı çağırıp karakter oluşturma ekranına dönüyor, `hp.current>0`'ken `GameOverScreen` hiç gösterilmiyor (regresyon)

**Bulunan ve coder tarafından anında düzeltilen bug (commit `a2d6bab`):** `TacticalGrid.tsx`'in `handleCellClick`'inde büyü hedef-seç modundayken (`castingSpellId` set) düşman OLMAYAN bir hücreye (kendi hücresi, boş kare) tıklamak `castSpell`'i çağırmıyordu ama sessizce `handleMoveTarget`'a düşüp `moveToken` çağırıyordu — yani "hedef seç" modundayken oyuncu yanlışlıkla hareket ettirilebiliyordu (throw modunda bu sorun yoktu, çünkü `throwingItemId` dalı erken `return` ediyor). Regresyon testiyle kırmızıya düşürüp raporladım, coder aynı oturumda no-op erken `return` ekleyerek düzeltti; test şimdi yeşil.

**Kritik altyapı bulgusu (bug değil, ortam/süreç sorunu — düzeltildi):** Canlı tarayıcı QA'sına başlayınca `/api/scene/cast` ve `/api/character/reset` **404 "Cannot POST"** döndürüyordu — backend test suite'i 216/216 yeşildi ama **çalışan dev server** (`node server.js`, watch modu değil) coder'ın Faz 6-C route'larını hiç yüklememiş eski bir process'ti (muhtemelen o commit'ten önce başlatılmış, restart edilmemiş). `netstat`+`taskkill` ile PID'i bulup `npm run dev` (`node --watch`) ile yeniden başlattım — sonrasında tüm endpoint'ler 200 döndü. **Ders:** frontend/backend "testleri yeşil" demek çalışan dev server'ın güncel kodu çalıştırdığı anlamına gelmiyor; büyük bir faz kapanışından önce dev server'ın gerçekten restart edildiğini doğrulamak gerekiyor.

- [x] **Tarayıcıda uçtan uca canlı QA (Playwright, restart edilmiş backend'e karşı, 2026-08-22):** Büyücü karakteri oluşturuldu → "Büyüler" bölümü doğru render edildi → İyileştir'e tıklandı, mana 12/12→8/12 düştü, HP güncellendi, sohbete anlatım düştü → Ateş Topu'na tıklanınca "Büyü hedefi seç" moduna girdi (grid'de düşman tıklanabilir hale geldi) → "Turu Bitir" ile tur döngüsü ilerletilip HP 0'a düşürüldü → **GameOverScreen doğru render edildi** ("QA Buyucu, seviye 1'de düştü") → "Yeniden Başla" tıklanınca `resetSession` çağrıldı ve **Karakter Oluştur formuna başarıyla dönüldü**. Konsol hatalarının hepsi beklenen (400'ler menzil-dışı/HP-0 sonrası reddedilen istekler, birkaç başlangıç 404'ü) — gerçek/beklenmeyen hata yok.

## Phase 7 — Content Variety + Deploy Prep (Postgres migration)

User decision: "keep implementing the innovation ideas." PM assessment: there's only a single fixed scene/encounter (winning doesn't advance anywhere), and the project still only runs on localhost. Researched deploy options: Render offers a real free tier without requiring a card, but free web services have NO persistent disk (the SQLite file can be wiped) — user decision: migrate to Render + free Postgres, keeping real persistence.

### A) Content variety (coder, can start right away) [x] COMPLETE (commit 1ff7458)
- [x] `data/encounters.js` — 4 different encounters (Abandoned Dungeon/goblin, Spider Den/giant spider, Skeleton Graveyard/2 skeletons, Dragon Lair/young dragon). `createScene(encounterIndex)` in `sceneFactory.js` sets up from this list (wraps back to the start at the end of the list). `/attack` and `/cast` call `advanceToNextEncounter()` once no enemies remain in the scene and add a transition message to the narration.
- [x] Progress indicator: `scene.encounterIndex`/`totalEncounters` — since the scene was already written entirely as JSON to the DB (Phase 6-B), no new DB schema was needed, it's automatically persistent. The frontend shows "Encounter: N/M".

**Verification:** end-to-end on an isolated port — when the goblin died the scene automatically advanced to "Spider Den" (new enemy, player returned to spawn, turn/action reset), `encounterIndex` was preserved in a real-file-DB restart test. backend 215/216 (1 known behavior change — see the commit message, tester needs to update), frontend 73/73 green.

### B) Migration to Postgres (coder, can be done in parallel, independent of A) [x] COMPLETE (commit 94e20de)
- [x] `data/dbSqlite.js` (synchronous, the existing `better-sqlite3` logic) and `data/dbPostgres.js` (asynchronous, `pg` Pool) provide the same method set. `data/db.js` picks: Postgres if `DATABASE_URL` is set AND `VITEST` is not, SQLite otherwise. `services/persistence.js`'s save* functions are still called as if synchronous (no changes needed in the routes) — genuinely blocking in SQLite, "fire and forget" in Postgres (the in-memory Map is already the runtime authority). `loadAll()` became async, `server.js` awaits it at startup.
- [x] `scripts/initPostgres.js` — a helper script for manual schema setup (the app already runs `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` automatically on every startup).

**Verification:** without `DATABASE_URL`, engine='sqlite' (unchanged); `DATABASE_URL`+`VITEST=true` → still 'sqlite' (test isolation preserved); `DATABASE_URL` alone → 'postgres', the `Pool` doesn't connect eagerly; a real startup attempt with a fake/unreachable Postgres URL exited cleanly with a clear error message (didn't hang); the end-to-end real-file-SQLite restart test still worked after the refactor. backend 237/237, no real Postgres instance available — that part was verified with the dry-run tests above, a real connection test will happen with the PM during the deploy phase.

### C) Render deploy prep (coder, code/config level) [x] COMPLETE (commit d2a6558)
- [x] `PORT` was already read from `process.env.PORT` (no change needed). `GEMINI_API_KEY`/`DATABASE_URL` were already read only from environment variables, `.env` is gitignored, no secrets in code/repo — verified.
- [x] `frontend/src/api.ts`: `BASE` is now read from `import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE` (falls back to `/api` if unset, local dev proxy behavior unchanged). Verified with a real build: with a build-time `VITE_API_BASE` env, the full URL gets embedded in the bundle.
- [x] `render.yaml` — backend (Node Web Service, `healthCheckPath: /api/health`, `DATABASE_URL` auto-wired from Render Postgres via `fromDatabase`, `GEMINI_API_KEY` `sync: false` meaning entered manually via the dashboard), frontend (Static Site, `VITE_API_BASE` tries to connect via `fromService` to the backend service), free Postgres definition.

**Note (coder, in the interest of honesty):** I couldn't 100% verify that `render.yaml`'s `fromService` cross-service full-URL reference matches Render's current schema exactly, since I don't have a real Render environment — I left an explicit comment in the file noting this, the PM/user may need to check this field on the first deploy attempt in the dashboard.

**IMPORTANT — coder/tester ATTENTION:** Setting up a real Render/GitHub account, pushing the repo to GitHub, and setting up the services in the Render dashboard will be handled separately between the PM and the user (external account actions, not within your authority). Your job is only to make sure the code/config is READY for deploy.

- [x] Verify the Postgres abstraction doesn't break SQLite behavior (existing tests must still pass, explicitly test that it falls back to SQLite when `DATABASE_URL` is absent). If there's no real Postgres instance, mock/document this part — a real Postgres connection test will happen with the PM at the deploy stage.

Moving to a new area: Spider Den.", and after the transition, trying to attack the same goblin-1 again correctly returned 404.

**Tester notes (B — Postgres abstraction, 2026-08-22):**
- Fixed the one known breakage the coder documented (`loadAll()` is now async): the 3 `loadAll()` calls in `persistence.test.js` were awaited, the relevant test callbacks made `async`. White-box tests (direct access via `db.exec`/`db.prepare`) needed no changes thanks to the raw `db` instance `dbSqlite.js` still exports for backward compatibility.
- `db.test.js` (new, 3 tests): does the engine selection work correctly based on env variables — testing this within the SAME process isn't reliable because `data/db.js` reads `process.env` AT IMPORT TIME and Node's CJS require cache isn't cleared by `vi.resetModules()`; instead I ran each scenario in a separate `node -e` subprocess and read the `engine` field (the only reliable way to test the actual "at import time" behavior). 3 verified scenarios: `engine==='sqlite'` when `DATABASE_URL` is absent, STILL `'sqlite'` with `DATABASE_URL`+`VITEST=true` (test isolation), `'postgres'` with `DATABASE_URL` alone (and the module does NOT crash at import time — confirms the `pg` Pool doesn't connect eagerly).
- Independently re-verified the coder's claim that "a real startup attempt with a fake Postgres URL exits cleanly": ran a real `node server.js` with `DATABASE_URL=postgres://...localhost:59999/...` — it printed a clear `ECONNREFUSED` error and exited cleanly with **exit code 1** (didn't hang).
- Live restart with a real file `game.db`: still worked after the refactor — the `node --watch` dev server auto-loaded the coder's saved changes, I confirmed a character I created and moved before the restart came back identically afterward.
- Test status: backend **237/237**, tsc not needed (backend-only).

**Tester notes (C — Render deploy prep, 2026-08-22):**
- Reviewed `render.yaml` — `healthCheckPath: /api/health` genuinely exists and works (verified with curl), `GEMINI_API_KEY` is genuinely `sync: false` (no secret in the repo), the structure makes sense. I agree with the coder's own note: whether the `fromService`/`hostport` field matches Render's current schema exactly can't be verified without a real account — this will be checked with the PM+user on the first deploy attempt, not within my/the coder's authority.
- Independently verified the `VITE_API_BASE` build-time behavior with two real `vite build` runs: with `VITE_API_BASE` set, the full URL (`onrender.com/api`) gets embedded in the bundle; without it, there's no `onrender` reference in the bundle at all (the default `/api` behavior is intact).
- Test status: frontend **75/75**, tsc+vite build (both default and with the `VITE_API_BASE` override) clean.

**Phase 7 (A+B+C) FULLY verified by the tester** — no known open bugs. Since there's no real Postgres/Render environment, part of B/C (a real connection, a real dashboard deploy) was verified via mock/dry-run — real verification will happen with the PM+user at the deploy stage.

## Phase 8 — Post-Deploy Feedback (bugs + AI quality + inventory UX + layout)

**DEPLOY STATUS:** The project is live: https://dnd-game-frontend-t9hr.onrender.com/ (backend: dnd-game-backend-sz9e.onrender.com, Postgres: dnd-game-db). The user tested it in the real environment and gave feedback.

**PM diagnosis (important, read before the coder starts):** In production, the AI Game Master **falls back to mock** (tried `/api/chat` with `curl`, it returned `source: "mock"`) — this is the root cause of the user's "the DM is dumb/repetitive" complaint, not the narration-quality code. The `GEMINI_API_KEY` value on Render is being checked (together with the user, the PM is also looking into it) — the coder can wait for the PM's "key fixed" confirmation before touching this item, or work in parallel on increasing the mock templates' variety (both are useful, neither blocks the other).

### A) Bugs (priority 1) [x] COMPLETE (coder)
- [x] **Automatic page-scroll bug**: the root cause was exactly as guessed — `bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'})` in `ChatPanel.tsx`, with its default `block:'start'` behavior, went past the nearest scrollable ancestor and scrolled the page too. `scrollIntoView` was removed entirely, replaced with a direct ref on the `.chat-messages` container and `container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight` (only that container scrolls, the page/body is never affected). Verified with Playwright: `document.scrollingElement.scrollTop` stayed at 0 after multiple chat messages/turns.

### B) AI/DM narration quality (priority 2) — key-independent parts [x] COMPLETE (coder)
- [ ] Prod verification with a real key — on the PM/user's side, not within the coder's scope
- [x] Increased mock template variety: `data/gmFlavor.js` — GENERIC 5→10, expanded the ATTACK/LOOK/TALK pools, added two new categories (movement/exploration, spells) + the regexes now catch more verb variations. The enemy attack/approach text in `services/enemyAI.js` is also no longer a single fixed sentence, now randomly picked from multiple templates (`pick()` helper).
- [x] **Hid raw dice math from combat messages**: `runEnemyTurn` in `services/enemyAI.js` no longer embeds text like "(18+2=20 vs 12)" in the message (narratively useful info like HP status stays, e.g. "(HP: 8/12)"). `/attack` and `/cast` already used AI/mock narration (never had raw math), and additionally an optional `roll` parameter was added to `pushGmMessage` and attached to the attackResult/castResult message (for debug/QA, present in the JSON). Frontend `ChatPanel.tsx`: removed the old `roll-tag` paragraph (a constantly-visible "STRENGTH check: 14+2=16 (DC 12)" under the main text), replaced with a small `roll-badge` chip that only shows the outcome (`Success`/`Failure` etc.) — the full numeric detail stays in the `title` tooltip. Verified live with Playwright (a full fight with a goblin, including death + encounter transition) that the `\d+\+\d+=\d+` pattern never appears anywhere in the chat text.
- **Known test breakage (intentional):** 2 tests in `backend/tests/enemyAI.test.js` and `scene.test.js` expected the FIXED "...hits you..." text for the enemy attack message — since it's now randomly picked from multiple templates, some runs now match a different template (e.g. "lands a solid blow"). 1 test in `frontend/ChatPanel.test.tsx` expects the old `roll-tag` text (raw "14+2=16"), there's now a `roll-badge` + tooltip instead.

### C) Inventory/equipment UX (priority 3) [x] COMPLETE (coder)
- [x] **Fixed the Use/Equip button confusion**: `CharacterCard.tsx` — the "Use" button is now shown ONLY when `!item.slot` (a slot-less/consumable item, e.g. a potion); "Equip/Unequip" is shown ONLY when `item.slot` exists. Verified with Playwright (Short Sword/Leather Armor → Equip only, Potion → Use only).
- [x] **Added drag-and-drop equipping**: inventory `<li>` items are now `draggable` (for items that have a slot and aren't equipped), the paper-doll's slots accept native HTML5 drag-and-drop via `onDragOver`/`onDrop` (if the item's `slot` doesn't match the target slot, the drop is silently ignored). Click-to-equip/unequip still works unchanged. **Note:** this required removing the `disabled` HTML attribute from empty slots (disabled elements don't receive drag/drop events) — click safety is now guarded by the `equippedItem &&` check inside `onClick`, and the CSS was updated to key off the `.filled` class instead of `:disabled`. Verified end-to-end in Playwright with a real HTML5 drag simulation (Short Sword → the "Hand" slot).
- [x] **Expanded emoji usage**: HP (❤️)/Mana (🔮)/Spells (✨)/Equipment (🎽)/Inventory (🎒) headings, each of the 13 paper-doll slots (🪖 Head, 😷 Mask, 👓 Glasses, 👂 Ears, 📿 Neck, 🎒 Back, 🥋 Suit, 👕 Under, 🧤 Gloves, 🪢 Belt, 👢 Shoes, 💍 Accessories, ⚔️ Hand), inventory action buttons (🧪 Use, 🎽 Equip/Unequip, 🗑️ Drop, 🎯 Throw).
- **Known test breakage (intentional):** 12 tests in `frontend/CharacterCard.test.tsx` expect the old button text (emoji-less "Use"/"Equip" etc.), the old "always two buttons" assumption, and the `disabled` attribute on an empty slot — all a deliberate UX change, tester needs to update.

### D) Layout (priority 4) [x] COMPLETE (coder, together with A — tied to the same root cause)
- [x] `.app` is now `height:100vh; overflow:hidden` (was `min-height:100vh`), `.app-main` is `flex:1; min-height:0; overflow:hidden`, the three panels (`character-card`/`tactical-grid`/`chat-panel`) scroll internally via `overflow-y:auto`. Added `html,body,#root{height:100%;overflow:hidden}` to `index.css` (defensive). Did NOT add a fully drag-and-drop-customizable panel layout (the user didn't want that), just a fixed/non-overflowing layout. Verified with Playwright at a 1400x800 viewport: `document.body.scrollHeight === window.innerHeight` (the page never overflows), `.character-card` scrolls internally (`scrollHeight > clientHeight`).

### Tester [x] COMPLETE (2026-08-23)

## Phase 9 — Implementing Creative-Cron Findings (rate-limit, cleanup, mobile, error UX)

Items 1-4 from the "Innovation Ideas" section below were evaluated by the PM and formalized as Phase 9.

### Coder [x] COMPLETE
- [x] **General API rate-limit** (idea #2): added `express-rate-limit` (`services/publicRateLimit.js`), applied to `/character/create` and `/character/roll-stats` — 20 requests per minute per IP (overridable via the `PUBLIC_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` env var), 429 + an error message once exceeded. Fully disabled in the test environment (`VITEST`) (otherwise tests would trip each other into 429s). **Note:** since the two routes share the same middleware instance, the counter is also shared — i.e. `/create` + `/roll-stats` together are subject to a combined 20-requests-per-minute limit (not separately), intentional as a "general" public-endpoint limit. Verified with a live curl test: of 25 consecutive requests, the first 20 were 200, the next 5 were 429.
- [x] **Orphan cleanup** (idea #1): `/character/reset` now genuinely deletes the orphaned character row (both the in-memory Map and the DB) via `persistence.clearSession(sessionId, characterId)`. Also added an `updated_at` column to the `sessions` table (SQLite: existence check via `PRAGMA table_info` + `ALTER TABLE`; Postgres: `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`), updated on every `upsertSession` call. Added `cleanupStaleSessions(maxAgeMs)` to `services/persistence.js` — deletes sessions older than 30 days (+ their linked character/scene/chat); `server.js` calls it once at startup and then once a day via `setInterval` (`.unref()`'d), never runs under `VITEST`. Verified via a live curl + a direct `game.db` query: after a reset, the character row is genuinely deleted from the DB (404 + an `undefined` row in the DB).
- [x] **Silent fetch errors** (idea #4): added a `NetworkError` class to `api.ts` — thrown when `fetch()` fails outright (a connection error) or the response can't be parsed as JSON (e.g. the raw error page Render returns on a cold start), kept separate from normal HTTP errors (e.g. the "no active character" 404). The initial character load in `App.tsx` now shows "Couldn't connect, retrying... (N/3)" ONLY on a `NetworkError` and automatically retries 3 times, 4 seconds apart; a real 404 (no active character) falls straight to the creation form with no retry at all. Verified live with Playwright: with the backend down, the retry message appears with the counter, and it automatically recovers once the backend comes back up.
- [x] **Basic mobile/responsive support** (idea #3, PM decision): added `@media (max-width: 768px)` to `App.css` — `.app-main` collapses to a single column (`grid-auto-rows: 42vh`, scrolls vertically within itself), `.character-creation`/`.intro-screen`/`.game-over-screen` switch from a fixed pixel width to `width:100%; max-width:420px`. No complex tabs/accordion added (consistent with the PM's decision). Verified in Playwright at a real 375×812 viewport: no horizontal overflow on any screen (`body.scrollWidth === innerWidth`), the 3 panels are accessible in vertical order, the character creation form is also properly centered.

### Tester

- [x] Test for orphan cleanup (verify the character is genuinely deleted after a reset, stale sessions are cleaned up)
- [x] Test/QA for the fetch retry UX (temporarily kill the backend and try seeing the retry message)
- [x] Visual QA of the mobile breakpoint in Playwright with a real narrow viewport (e.g. 375px)
- [x] **Known test breakage (intentional):** the "reset does NOT delete the character itself" test in `backend/tests/scene.test.js` is now wrong — reset now GENUINELY deletes the character (that was the whole point of idea #1). The test's name and assertions need to be flipped (to expect `404`).

An unrelated endpoint (`GET /character`) was unaffected (returned a normal 404, wasn't rate-limited).

(1/3)" → "(2/3)" → "(3/3)" appeared correctly in sequence, ~4s apart (confirmed with a screenshot, properly centered, no layout issue). ## Phase 10 — Equipment Mechanic (armor/weapons now affect combat)

The user-decision-formalized version of innovation idea #18. User decisions: armor gives **flat damage reduction** (not AC/harder-to-hit), weapons give **different damage dice**.

### Coder [x] COMPLETE (no tester, self-tested/live-verified by the coder)
- [x] **Weapon damage die**: `backend/data/weaponDamage.js` (new) — `getWeaponDamageDie(name)`: `"Short Sword":6, "Dagger x2":4, "Mace":6, "Wand":4` (default 4 for unarmed, 6 for a weapon not on the map/unknown). **Note:** used the full item name `"Dagger x2"` instead of the spec's `"Dagger"` (the Rogue's actual starting item is recorded under this name in `data/dnd.js` — `itemSlots.js` also uses the same full name, for consistency). `/attack` in `routes/scene.js` now, instead of the fixed `ATTACK_DAMAGE_DIE=6`, finds the item equipped in the player's `hand` slot (`slot==="hand" && equipped===true`) and picks the die based on its name; the now-dead `ATTACK_DAMAGE_DIE` constant was removed.
- [x] **Armor damage reduction**: `backend/data/armorReduction.js` (new) — `getTotalArmorReduction(character)`: sums the reduction of items equipped in the `suit`/`back` slots. `"Leather Armor":2, "Old Shield":1` added as specified, also added `"Shield":1` (**finding:** "Old Shield" is actually a loot item — `data/encounters.js` — and loot pickup currently never gets added to the inventory at all, the backend returns `collectedLoot` but the frontend never uses it, a separate pre-existing gap — meaning "Old Shield" can practically never be equipped; the Cleric's ACTUALLY equippable starting item is "Shield", added so the armor mechanic is meaningful for the Cleric too). `runEnemyTurn` in `services/enemyAI.js` applies `Math.max(0, rawDamage - totalReduction)` after computing raw damage, the POST-reduction damage is what goes into `hitMessage` (the text shown to the user).
- [x] Checked for conflicts with narration/mock templates: `enemyAI.js`'s message templates already use the post-reduction `damage` variable (no code change needed); `/attack`'s (player attack) AI/mock narration hasn't included raw numbers since Phase 8 anyway.

**Verification (no tester, done by the coder):** 16 new tests added — `tests/weaponDamage.test.js` (6, pure module tests), `tests/armorReduction.test.js` (7, pure module tests), 4 new tests added to `tests/enemyAI.test.js` (armored/unarmored/reduction-clamped-at-0/doesn't-crash-without-inventory), a "Phase 10: damage die based on equipped weapon" block added to `tests/scene.test.js` (2 tests: Short Sword vs Dagger x2 different damage, unarmed defaults to d4). The 10 existing `/attack` tests were deliberately updated (they now explicitly equip a Short Sword to preserve the old d6 math — since characters start unarmed/unequipped by default, the old tests had unknowingly started testing the "unarmed d4" scenario). Also verified live with curl: on a critical hit, a 2×d6 sum (10, within the valid 2-12 range) was observed; for a character with Leather Armor equipped, HP drops across consecutive enemy attacks (4, 2, 1) were always consistent with raw d6 values minus the reduction. Backend **255/255**, frontend **80/80** (unaffected), tsc+build clean.

### Tester (or self-verified by the coder) [x] done by the coder
- [x] Verify that damage ranges genuinely differ between different weapons (Dagger x2 vs Short Sword)
- [x] Verify that damage is reduced for a character with armor equipped on an enemy attack, and not reduced for one without
- [x] Verify it doesn't go below 0 (no negative damage/heal)

## Phase 11 — Major UI Redesign: Text-First Experience, Grid Only During Combat

Direct feedback from the user (2026-08-24): the current always-visible 3-panel (character/chat/map) layout feels like "a straight-up versus fighting game," what's wanted is a classic D&D tabletop RP experience (text-heavy). User decisions became clear:

### Coder — sırayla
- [x] **The main view is now full-screen text/chat** — the always-visible 3-column grid (`.app-main`) in `App.tsx` is removed, and when there is NO enemy in the encounter (`scene.tokens.some(t=>t.type==="enemy")` false), only `ChatPanel` is shown full-screen.
- [x] **The tactical grid should now automatically appear only WHEN there is an enemy in the encounter** — the existing scene data already knows this (whether `scene.tokens` has any with `type==="enemy"`), no need to keep an extra "combat mode" state, derive it directly from the scene data. Once enemies are cleared (the encounter transition already triggers this), it should automatically return to full-screen text.
- [x] **The character card is no longer a permanently visible column** — there should be a small icon/button in a corner (e.g. in the header); clicking it opens the character info/inventory (the existing CharacterCard content) as a panel/modal, and clicking again closes it. This button should remain accessible while the grid is showing too (inventory should be usable during combat as well — use/equip/throw items).
- [x] **Layout transitions (text↔grid) should not be abrupt, they should use a smooth CSS transition/fade** — related to the user's "no animation" complaint, this transition is at least a start.
- [x] The existing mobile breakpoint (Phase 9) should be re-evaluated for this new layout — full-screen text should already be simpler on mobile, but it needs testing.

**[2026-08-24] [x] DÜZELTİLDİ (coder) — ana layout değişikliği tamamlandı, ARADA bir mimari çelişki keşfedilip PM'e danışıldı ve çözüldü.** `App.tsx`'teki sabit 3 sütunlu (`grid-template-columns: 280px 1fr 380px`) düzen tamamen kaldırıldı; artık `.app-main` flex tabanlı, `mode-text`/`mode-combat` sınıflarıyla sürülüyor (`showGrid = hasEnemies || throw/cast hedef-seçimi`). `TacticalGrid` HER ZAMAN mount kalıyor (unmount/remount değil) — görünürlüğü sadece CSS ile (`flex-basis`/`width`/`opacity`/`padding`/`border-width` transition, 0.25-0.3s) yönetiliyor, böylece hem geçiş animasyonlu oluyor hem grid kendi fetch/poll yaşam döngüsünü kaybetmiyor. Karakter kartı artık header'daki 🎒 butonuyla açılıp kapanan bir sol-drawer overlay (`.character-panel-overlay`/`.character-panel`, fade-in+slide-in-left animasyonlu) — grid görünürken de erişilebilir, savaşta da envanter/fırlatma/büyü aksiyonları çalışıyor. Mobil breakpoint (768px) yeni 2-panelli (chat+grid) modele göre yeniden yazıldı (savaş modunda dikey stack, 45vh/45vh).

**Mimari çelişki (coder → PM → çözüm, "nefes alma penceresi"):** İlk implementasyonda "grid sadece düşman varken görünsün" kuralı ile Faz 7-A'nın "sonsuz zindan" tasarımı (bir karşılaşmanın son düşmanı ölünce sıradaki karşılaşmanın düşmanları AYNI response'ta anında sahneye giriyordu) çelişiyordu — pratikte grid neredeyse hiç kaybolmuyordu (bir karşılaşma bitince zaten bir sonrakinin düşmanı anında beliriyordu). Coder bunu QA sırasında fark edip PM'e 3 seçenekle escalate etti; PM "nefes alma penceresi" yaklaşımını seçti: karşılaşma temizlenince yeni karşılaşma HEMEN kurulmuyor, sahne `pendingEncounterIndex` ile işaretlenip düşmansız bırakılıyor, asıl geçiş oyuncunun SIRADAKİ eylemine kadar erteleniyor.

Backend (`backend/routes/scene.js`): `checkEncounterCleared(scene)` artık `advanceToNextEncounter`'ı hemen çağırmıyor — sadece `scene.pendingEncounterIndex = clearedIndex + 1` işaretleyip anlatıma "Alanı temizledin, ilerliyorsun..." (ya da tam tur tamamlandıysa özel tebrik cümlesi) ekliyor. Yeni `resolvePendingEncounter(sessionId, scene, character, res)`, `POST /move`'un en başında (koordinat doğrulamasından ÖNCE) devreye giriyor: bekleyen bir geçiş varsa oyuncunun hareket isteğini normal hedef-koordinat mantığıyla hiç işlemeden "yeni alana giriş" olarak yorumluyor (`advanceToNextEncounter` + taze anlatım + `pendingEncounterIndex = null`). `sceneFactory.js`'in `createScene()`'ine `pendingEncounterIndex: null` alanı eklendi.

Frontend tarafında ilk yazımda TacticalGrid'i `pendingEncounterIndex` sırasında da görünür tutup bir hücreye tıklamayı geçiş tetikleyicisi yapmıştım — ama bu, PM'in "oyuncu gerçekten bir-iki hamlelik düşmansız/metin-modu bir an yaşıyor" tarifiyle çelişiyordu (grid görünürse hiçbir zaman tam-metin moduna dönülmüyordu, orijinal Faz 11 hedefini pratikte boşa çıkarıyordu). Bunu kendim yakalayıp düzelttim: `App.tsx`'te `hasEnemies` artık SADECE `scene.tokens.some(enemy)`'den türüyor (pending durumu karıştırmıyor), ayrı bir `pendingEncounter` state'i eklendi. Bekleyen geçiş sırasında (`pendingEncounter && !showGrid`) `.app-main` üzerinde ortalanmış küçük bir "Alanı temizledin, ilerliyorsun... [Devam Et]" banner'ı beliriyor — buton, backend'in koordinatlara bakmadan devreye giren `resolvePendingEncounter`'ını tetiklemek için `moveToken('player', 0, 0)` çağırıyor (grid'e tıklamaya gerek yok, tam-metin modundayken de çalışıyor). Böylece gerçek bir tam-ekran metin anı (grid tamamen gizli, karakter paneli hâlâ erişilebilir) yaşanıyor, sonra "Devam Et" ile bir sonraki karşılaşmaya "yürüyerek" geçiliyor.

**Doğrulama:** Backend `checkEncounterCleared`/`resolvePendingEncounter`/`/move` testleri yeni iki-adımlı davranışa göre yeniden yazıldı (6 test güncellendi) — **262/262** backend testi geçiyor. Frontend: `TacticalGrid.test.tsx`'teki sahne fixture'ına `pendingEncounterIndex` eklendi, `App.test.tsx` regresyon göstermedi — **80/80** frontend testi geçiyor, tsc+build temiz. Canlı Playwright QA (`qa_faz11_breather.js`) tam döngüyü doğruladı: son düşmanı öldür → sahne bekleyen geçişe girer (encounterIndex sabit, düşman yok, `pendingEncounterIndex` set) → sayfa `mode-text`'e geçer (grid tamamen gizli, tam ekran sohbet) → "Devam Et" banner'ı görünür → karakter paneli bu sırada da açılıp kapanabiliyor (🎒 buton çalışıyor) → "Devam Et"e tıklanınca yeni karşılaşma (yeni düşman, spawn'a dönen oyuncu, taze tur/aksiyon) yükleniyor ve `mode-combat`'a geçiliyor. Konsol hatası yok (sadece bilinen/ilgisiz Gemini kota-aşımı → mock fallback logu, önceden var olan bir durum).

### Coder — animasyon/his iyileştirmeleri (ayrı, küçük parçalar halinde ele alınabilir)
- [x] Saldırı/hasar anlarında görsel bir tepki (örn. hedef token'da kısa bir "sallanma"/flaş CSS animasyonu, hasar sayısının belirip kaybolduğu bir "damage popup") — **[2026-08-25] [x] DÜZELTİLDİ (coder, kapsam: sadece oyuncunun `/attack`'ı, düşman turu otomatik saldırıları kapsam dışı)** `TacticalGrid.tsx`'e `damageFx` state'i eklendi (`{x,y,damage,key}`) — saldırı isabet edip hasar>0 dönünce hedefin (ölse bile ölmeden ÖNCEKİ) koordinatında 700ms süren bir `damage-flash` (sallanma+kırmızı flaş, `App.css`'teki `damage-shake`/`damage-flash-bg` keyframe'leri) ve üzerinde yükselip kaybolan bir `-N` `damage-popup` (`damage-popup-rise` keyframe) tetikleniyor. Hedef öldürülüp sahneden kalksa bile koordinat önceden yakalandığından popup doğru karede beliriyor. Playwright ile canlı doğrulandı: gerçek bir saldırı isabet ettiğinde `.damage-popup` doğru hasar değerini (`-4`) gösterdi. Düşmanın oyuncuya vurduğu otomatik tur (`/end-turn`) şu an yapısal olarak token bazlı hasar döndürmüyor (sadece anlatım metni) — o yüzden bu ilk pas SADECE oyuncunun kendi saldırısını kapsıyor, düşman-oyuncuya-vuruş efekti backend'e ayrı bir alan eklemeyi gerektirir, kapsam dışı bırakıldı. Frontend testleri regresyon göstermedi (80/80), tsc+build temiz.
- [x] Token hareketi anlık "zıplama" yerine CSS transition ile kareler arası kayarak hareket etsin (grid göründüğünde) — **[2026-08-25] [x] DÜZELTİLDİ (coder) — orta ölçekli refactor.** Önceden her token, üzerinde durduğu grid hücresinin (`.cell`) kendi arka planı/harfiydi — bir hücreden diğerine "geçiş" diye bir şey yoktu, farklı bir hücre anında token rengini/harfini alıyordu. **Yaklaşım:** Token'lar artık `.grid`'in üzerine bindirilmiş ayrı bir `.token-layer` overlay katmanında, `ResizeObserver` ile ölçülmüş GERÇEK piksel konumlarına (`left`/`top`, `transition: left/top 0.3s ease`) yerleştiriliyor — yüzde bazlı bir hesap `gap:2px`/`padding` yüzünden hücre sınırlarıyla tam örtüşmeyeceğinden (küçük ama birikimli bir kayma olurdu) gerçek `getBoundingClientRect` ölçümü kullanıldı. `.cell` butonları artık SADECE engel/loot görselini ve tıklama/erişilebilirlik (title/aria-label, token varsa hâlâ doğru isim+HP) taşıyor, token rengi/harfi kaldırıldı (overlay tek görsel kaynak). Token id'si aynı kalıp sadece x/y değiştiğinde (oyuncu hareketi, düşman AI hareketi) React aynı DOM node'unu koruyor, CSS transition kayarak oynatıyor; yeni bir karşılaşmaya geçilince (farklı token id'leri) React'in key bazlı reconciliation'ı eskiyi kaldırıp yenisini konumunda direkt mount ediyor (istenmeyen bir "önceki karşılaşmadan kayma" olmuyor). Saldırı/hasar shake-flash+damage popup (bir önceki madde) kasıtlı olarak KOORDİNAT bazlı bırakıldı (token overlay'inden bağımsız) — hedef ölüp overlay'den kalksa bile efekt doğru karede görünmeye devam ediyor. `jsdom` (test ortamı) `ResizeObserver` sağlamadığından bir `typeof` koruması eklendi (gerçek tarayıcıda tam ölçüm+takip, testte ilk-mount ölçümü yeterli). Playwright ile canlı doğrulandı: overlay token'ı doğru piksel konumunda render ediyor (`getComputedStyle` ile `left`/`top` + `transition: left 0.3s, top 0.3s` teyit edildi), hareket sonrası konum doğru güncelleniyor, `.cell.player-token`/`.cell.enemy-token` artık hiçbir hücrede yok (overlay tek kaynak, çift-görsel yok), ekran görüntüsüyle de doğru göründüğü teyit edildi. Frontend testleri regresyon göstermedi (80/80 — mevcut testler `.cell` sırasını/`title`'ını kullanıyordu, ikisi de değişmedi), tsc+build temiz.
- [x] Chat'e yeni bir GM/oyuncu mesajı geldiğinde satırın yumuşak bir fade-in ile belirmesi — **[2026-08-25] [x] DÜZELTİLDİ (coder)** `App.css`'teki `.chat-message`'a mevcut `@keyframes fade-in` (0.3s ease) uygulandı. Ekstra bir "yeni mi eski mi" state'i gerekmedi — React'in `key={m.id}` bazlı reconciliation'ı zaten sadece gerçekten yeni mesaj DOM node'larını mount ediyor, var olan mesajlar (refetch/refreshKey değişince) aynı key ile kalıp yeniden mount/fade olmuyor. Playwright ile canlı doğrulandı: bir mesaj gönderilip DOM node'ları işaretlendikten sonra ikinci bir mesaj gönderilince, işaretli ESKİ node'lar aynen yerinde kaldı (remount olmadı, gereksiz re-flash yok), sadece yeni mesajlar eklendi. Frontend testleri regresyon göstermedi (80/80), tsc+build temiz.

- Mobil (375×812, gerçek Playwright viewport): yatay taşma yok (`body.scrollWidth <= innerWidth`), 🎒 butonu görünür ve tıklanabilir, karakter paneli tam ekran düzgün açılıyor, savaş görünümü (header/sohbet/grid) tek sütun halinde düzgün alt alta diziliyor — ekran görüntüsüyle doğrulandı, kırık layout yok.
- Konsol hatası yok (sadece beklenen başlangıç 404'leri). Not: erken denemelerimde ekipman kuşanmadan dövüşe giren zayıf bir test karakteri RNG'ye yenilip öldü — gerçek bir bug değildi, sadece test metodolojim; ekipmanlı ikinci denemede sorunsuz tamamlandı.
- Sonuç PM'e bildirildi, coder'a push için yeşil ışık verildi.

## Faz 12 — Serbest-Form Mimari Sıfırlaması (kullanıcı kararı, 2026-08-26)

**Karar:** Kullanıcı, Questsmith (thequestsmith.com) ve AI Dungeon'dan ilham alarak taktik grid tabanlı etkileşim modelinin tamamen kaldırılıp Questsmith/AI Dungeon tarzı **serbest-form (grid'siz, menüsüz) bir deneyime** geçilmesini istedi — "tam mimari sıfırlama" seçildi (görsel/UI yenilemeden daha büyük, ama grid'siz bir deneyime geçiş). **AI görsel/video üretimi İSTENMEDİ** — maliyet riski nedeniyle (kullanıcının Claude yerine Gemini'yi seçme sebebiyle aynı gerekçe) sadece metin AI'da kalıyor, bu kapsam dışı.

**Neyin kalacağı / neyin gideceği (PM analizi):**
- **KALACAK:** karakter oluşturma (ırk/sınıf/görünüş/stat), HP/mana/level/XP veri modeli, envanter/eşya veri modeli (artık grid-taktik değil, anlatımsal), persistence (SQLite/Postgres), session izolasyonu, AI entegrasyonu (Gemini+mock fallback+timeout+maxTokens), rate-limit'ler, güvenlik düzeltmeleri (CORS/IDOR/trust-proxy vb.), `services/actionResolver.js`'in D20+DC çözümleme mantığı (zaten `/chat`'te serbest metin için kısmen kullanılıyor — bu Faz'ın temel taşı).
- **GİDECEK (kademeli):** `TacticalGrid.tsx`, grid tabanlı token/x-y/engel/hareket sistemi, `/scene/move` (grid hedefi anlamında), grid'e bağlı Aksiyon/Bonus Aksiyon ekonomisi, grid'e özel görsel efektler (damage-flash/token-slide/aoe-preview — anlamlarını kaybedecek).
- **YENİ:** `/chat`'teki `resolveAction()` çözümlemesi GERÇEK mekanik sonuçlar üretecek şekilde genişletilecek (hasar/HP değişimi, XP/level, eşya bulma/kullanma) — oyuncu "Goblin'e saldırıyorum" yazınca gerçekten D20 atılıp gerçekten hasar/HP düşecek, şu an sadece anlatımsal.

**Yaklaşım:** Büyük patlama (big-bang) rewrite yerine kademeli/test edilebilir adımlar — mevcut grid sistemi yeni sistem kanıtlanana kadar SİLİNMEYECEK, sonra kaldırılacak (kullanılmayan attack surface'ı silme konusunda zaten iyi bir disiplinimiz var, bkz. fikir #29/#63).

### Faz 12-A (coder, ilk adım — atandı) — [x] TAMAMLANDI (2026-08-26, coder kendi test/QA'sı)
- [x] Backend: `/chat`'in `resolveAction()`/`actionResolver.js` çözümlemesini GERÇEK mekanik sonuçlara bağla — serbest metinde saldırı niyeti algılanınca (basit bir düşman state'i üzerinden, x/y'siz) gerçekten hasar uygulansın, düşman ölürse XP/level, eşya bulma/kullanma niyeti algılanınca envantere gerçekten eklensin/kullanılsın. Mevcut grid endpoint'lerine DOKUNMA (henüz kaldırma), sadece `/chat` yanında paralel çalışsın.
- [x] Basit bir "aktif düşman" scene state'i tasarla (x/y/obstacle YOK, sadece isim/HP/maxHP) — mevcut `encounters.js`'teki düşman verisini kaynak olarak kullanabilir.
- [x] Backend testleriyle kapsamlı doğrula, canlıda gerçek bir "saldırıyorum" mesajıyla gerçek HP değişimini doğrula.

**Tasarım (PM onaylı, bkz. cross-session mesajları):** Yeni `services/freeformEncounter.js` — `data/encounters.js`'in AYNI havuzunu kaynak alan, x/y/obstacle içermeyen `{encounterIndex, name, totalEncounters, enemies:[{id,name,hp,maxHp}], loot:[{id,name}]}` şeklinde bir sessionId→state Map'i. Bilinçli olarak DB'ye PERSİST EDİLMİYOR (grid hâlâ paralel/persist ediliyor, şekil Faz 12-B'de değişebilir, Faz 12-C'de grid kalkınca tek seferde doğru bir kalıcılık eklenecek) — sunucu restart'ında serbest-form ilerlemenin sıfırlanması bu geçiş fazında kabul edilebilir.

**Mekanik:** Yeni `services/freeformCombat.js` — saldırı niyeti (`actionResolver.js`'e eklenen `isAttackIntent`, mevcut `STR_PATTERN`'i yeniden kullanıyor) algılanınca `routes/scene.js`'teki `/attack`'in BİREBİR AYNI D20+silah hasarı+kritik+XP/level mantığını uygular (primary attribute üzerinden, hedef metinden isim eşleşmesiyle ya da ilk canlı düşman); eşya kullan (`isConsumeIntent`) envanterdeki bir iksiri gerçekten tüketip `/item/use`'daki aynı +5 HP mantığını uygular; eşya al (`isPickupIntent`) sahnedeki loot'u gerçekten envantere ekler (`MAX_INVENTORY` dahil aynı üst sınır). `/chat`'e entegre edilirken narrasyon metnine gerçek sonucu anlatan bir Türkçe ek (`describeFreeformResult`) ekleniyor (hasar/yenilgi/seviye/alan geçişi/eşya). `POST /character/reset` artık `resetFreeformEncounter()` de çağırıyor.

**Test:** Yeni `backend/tests/freeformCombat.test.js` (8 test) — gerçek hasar uygulama, düşman yenilgisi+XP+bir sonraki karşılaşmaya geçiş, iksir tüketme+HP iyileştirme, loot toplama, karakter/düşman yokken sessizce hiçbir mekanik sonuç üretmeme, `/character/reset`'in state'i sıfırlaması, ve grid'in KENDİ sahnesinin (ayrı Map) hiç etkilenmediği. Backend testleri **274/274** (266 eskiden + 8 yeni), regresyon yok.

**Canlı doğrulama (gerçek çalışan `server.js`, gerçek HTTP, encoding sorunlarını fark edip UTF-8 dosya ile düzelterek):** Gerçek bir karakter oluşturup arka arkaya "Goblin'e saldırıyorum!" mesajları gönderdim — gerçek hasar ilerlemesi (`Goblin'e 3 hasar verdin.` → ... → `Goblin yenildi! Alanı temizledin! Yeni alan: Örümcek İni.`), ikinci bir düşmanı da (Dev Örümcek) yenip `İskelet Mezarlığı`'na geçtim — `GET /api/character` ile **xp:40** (2 kill × 20 XP, tam beklenen) gerçekten doğrulandı. Ardından "Yerdeki eşyayı alıyorum" ile gerçek bir loot'un ("Eski Kalkan") envantere eklendiğini, "İksiri içiyorum" ile potion'ın gerçekten tüketilip envanterden kalktığını `GET /api/character` ile doğruladım. Son olarak `GET /api/scene` ile grid'in KENDİ sahnesinin (`Terk Edilmiş Mahzen`, goblin hâlâ 10/10 HP) serbest-form savaşından TAMAMEN etkilenmediğini doğruladım — iki sistem gerçekten paralel çalışıyor. Geçici sunucu/dosyalar temizlendi.

#### Tester (bağımsız QA, 2026-08-26, PM tarafından atandı)
- [x] Backend testlerini tekrar çalıştırdım: **274/274** doğrulandı, coder'ın iddiasıyla eşleşiyor.
- [x] Canlı sunucuda (izole port, geçici session) mutlu yol senaryolarını (saldırı→hasar, düşman ölümü→XP/karşılaşma geçişi, iksir tüketme, loot toplama, grid'in etkilenmemesi) coder'ın raporuyla tutarlı buldum.
- [x] **BUG BULUNDU (orta-yüksek önem) → [x] DÜZELTİLDİ (coder, 2026-08-26): `isPickupIntent`'teki `"al"` kökü çapasız/çok kısa — çok yaygın Türkçe kelimeleri yanlışlıkla "eşya al" niyeti olarak algılayıp GERÇEK bir envanter mutasyonu tetikliyordu.** `actionResolver.js`'deki `PICKUP_PATTERN`, `WORD_START` ile sadece kelime BAŞINI çapalıyor (kelime sonu çapası yok, bu Faz 3.5'ten beri bilinçli bir tasarım — eklemeli dil yüzünden), ama `"al"` iki harfli ve "al-" ile başlayan HERHANGİ bir kelimeyle eşleşiyor. Canlı olarak doğrulandı: oyuncu **"Altına bakıyorum"** (bir şeyin altına bakmak, roleplay) yazınca sistem sessizce bir loot'u ("Altın Kese") envantere ekledi ve "Altın Kese envanterine eklendi!" mesajı gösterdi — oyuncu bunu hiç istemedi. Node ile izole test edilince `isPickupIntent()` şu masum cümlelerin HEPSİNDE `true` dönüyor: "Altına bakıyorum", "Alacakaranlıkta yürüyorum", "Almanya'dan geldim", "Alev topunu inceliyorum", "Alkışlıyorum", "Alışveriş yapıyorum". Faz 12 öncesi bu tarz kısa kök sorunları (bkz. Faz 3.5'teki `"ara"`/`"it"` bulguları) sadece anlatım kategorisini yanlış seçtiriyordu (düşük risk) — ama bu artık GERÇEK state mutasyonu (envantere eşya ekleme, sahneden loot silme) tetikliyor, risk seviyesi değişti. **Ek not (aynı sınıf, düşük önem):** `STR_PATTERN`'deki `"kır"` kökü de "kırmızı" gibi kelimelerle yanlışlıkla eşleşip artık GERÇEK bir saldırı zarı atıyor (canlı doğrulandı: "Kırmızı pelerinimi çıkarıyorum" → gerçek D20 saldırı zarı atıldı) — bu kök Faz 3-D'den beri var ama önceden sadece flavor-text seçiyordu, Faz 12-A onu da gerçek mekaniğe bağladı.
  **Öneri:** `PICKUP_PATTERN`'e en az 3 harfli/daha spesifik kökler kullanmak (`"alıyor"`, `"topluyor"`, `"kaldırıyor"` gibi çekim-farkında varyantlar, ya da kelime SONU çapası da eklemek — ama bu eklemeli dil yapısını bozar, dikkatli tasarlanmalı) ve `STR_PATTERN`'deki `"kır"` için benzer bir gözden geçirme. Bu bir fix PR'ı gerektirir, ben (tester) kod değiştirmedim — PM'e raporluyorum.
- [x] Test durumu: backend **274/274** (regresyon yok, yeni bug için henüz otomatik test yazılmadı — coder/PM fix kapsamını belirleyince eklenecek).

**Faz 12-A tester QA'sı TAMAMLANDI — 1 orta-yüksek önem bug bulundu (yukarıda), coder'ın kendi QA'sında yakalanmamış. Faz 12-B'ye geçiş PM kararına bağlı (bu bug 12-B'yi bloke etmeyebilir ama gerçek kullanıcı deneyimini bozuyor, öncelik verilmesi önerilir).**

#### Coder fix (2026-08-26, Faz 12-B'den hemen sonra, PM'in atadığı öncelik)

**Düzeltme:** Tester'ın bulgusunu bağımsız olarak Node ile doğruladıktan sonra, AYNI sınıftan üçüncü bir bug da kendim buldum (tester henüz test etmemişti): `CONSUME_PATTERN`'deki `"iç"` (2 harf) kökü de "içeri"/"içinde"/"içimden" gibi son derece yaygın kelimelerle çakışıp gerçek bir iksir tüketimini tetikliyordu — normal roleplay metninde ("odanın içine giriyorum") bile sessizce potion harcanabiliyordu, bu üçü içinde en riskli olanıydı (en sık geçen kelime kalıbı). Üç deseni de (`STR_PATTERN`'deki `"kır"`, `PICKUP_PATTERN`'deki `"al"`, `CONSUME_PATTERN`'deki `"iç"`) bare kısa kökler yerine spesifik/çekim-farkında formlarla değiştirdim: `kırıyor/kırarım/kıracağım/kırdım` + emir kipi için özel bir kelime-SONU çapalı `kır(?![harf])` (Kapıyı kır" gibi ek almayan emir kipini kaybetmemek için — mevcut `"kapıyı kır" -> str` testi bunu zaten bekliyordu); `alıyor/alırım/alacağım/aldım` (+ mevcut `topla/toplu/kaldır/pick up`); `içiyor/içerim/içeceğim/içtim` (+ mevcut `kullan/tüket/drink`). `backend/tests/actionResolver.test.js`'e tester'ın TAM repro'sunu (Altına bakıyorum/Alacakaranlıkta/Almanya/Alkışlıyorum/Alışveriş, Kırmızı pelerinim, + kendi bulduğum İçeri/İçinde/İçimden) VE gerçek niyetlerin hâlâ çalıştığını (saldırıyorum, kapıyı kır emir kipi, alıyorum/topluyorum, içiyorum/kullanıyorum) doğrulayan 6 yeni test eklendi. Backend testleri **280/280** (274 eskiden + 6 yeni), regresyon yok (mevcut `"kapıyı kır" -> str` testi de dahil).

**Canlı doğrulama (gerçek çalışan `server.js`, tester'ın TAM repro'sunu tekrarlayarak):** Gerçek bir karakter oluşturup "Altına bakıyorum" gönderdim — `GET /api/character` ile envanterin DEĞİŞMEDİĞİNİ doğruladım (öncesi/sonrası aynı 3 eşya, tester'ın bulduğu "Altın Kese sessizce eklendi" davranışı artık yok). "Kırmızı pelerinimi çıkarıyorum" gönderdim — `gmMessage.roll.attribute` **"dex"** (varsayılan fallback) döndü, **"str" DEĞİL** (gerçek saldırı artık tetiklenmiyor). Son olarak gerçek bir saldırı ("Goblin'e saldırıyorum") gönderip `attribute:"str"` ile GERÇEK mekaniğin hâlâ çalıştığını da doğruladım (fix aşırıya kaçıp true positive'leri kırmamış). Geçici sunucu/dosyalar temizlendi.

### Faz 12-B (coder, atandı) — [x] TAMAMLANDI (2026-08-26, coder kendi test/QA'sı)
- [x] Frontend: Chat, oyunun varsayılan/tek arayüzü olsun — grid'i ZORUNLU açan mantığı kaldır (ama dosyayı henüz silme).
- [x] HP/Mana/Level gibi temel durumun her zaman görünür kalması (küçük bir HUD/şerit).

**Tasarım (PM onaylı, bkz. cross-session mesajları):** `App.tsx`'teki `showGrid = hasEnemies || Boolean(throwingItemId || castingSpellId)` formülündeki `hasEnemies` (sahnede düşman var mı) kaldırılıp yerine yeni bir `manualGridOpen` state'i (varsayılan `false`, header'daki yeni "⚔️ Harita" toggle butonuyla açılıp kapanıyor) geldi — chat artık HER ZAMAN varsayılan görünüm, grid hiçbir zaman kendiliğinden açılmıyor. `throwingItemId`/`castingSpellId` (büyü/eşya fırlatma hedef-seçimi) hâlâ formülde bilinçli olarak KALDI — Faz 12-A'nın freeform sistemi henüz cast/throw'u kapsamadığından (sadece saldırı/eşya-al/eşya-kullan), bu iki mekanik hâlâ grid'e muhtaç; kaldırılsaydı oyuncular mevcut büyü/fırlatma işlevselliğini tamamen kaybederdi. `TacticalGrid.tsx` dosyası ve grid-özel backend route'ları hiç değiştirilmedi/silinmedi (Faz 12-C'ye bırakıldı).

**HUD:** Yeni `components/HeaderHud.tsx` — header'da HER ZAMAN görünen küçük bir şerit (❤️ HP, mana varsa 🔮 Mana, Lv Seviye). Karakter panelini (🎒) açmaya gerek kalmadan temel durum görünür kalıyor; detaylı envanter/attributes/XP bar'ı için panel hâlâ mevcut, değiştirilmedi.

**Test:** `App.test.tsx`'e 3 yeni test — (1) sahnede düşman OLSA BİLE `.app-main`'in `mode-text` sınıfında kaldığını (eski `hasEnemies→showGrid` mantığının gerçekten kaldırıldığını) doğruluyor, (2) "Harita" toggle butonuna gerçek bir tıklamayla grid'in açılıp kapandığını (`mode-combat`↔`mode-text` sınıf değişimi) doğruluyor, (3) HUD'un karakter paneli hiç açılmadan HP/Mana/Seviye'yi gösterdiğini doğruluyor. Frontend testleri **89/89** (86 eskiden + 3 yeni), `oxlint` temiz, `tsc -b && vite build` temiz.

**Canlı doğrulama:** Bu oturumda tarayıcı otomasyon aracı (Playwright vb.) mevcut değildi — doğrulama React Testing Library'nin GERÇEK DOM render'ı + GERÇEK `userEvent.click()` tıklamaları üzerinden yapıldı (mock'lanmış bir olay değil, gerçek React state güncellemesi ve gerçek CSS sınıf değişimi doğrulandı). Ayrıca `npx vite` ile gerçek dev sunucusunu başlatıp `curl` ile `200` yanıtı ve konsol/başlangıç hatası olmadığını doğruladım (sözdizimi/modül hatası yok). Tam görsel tarayıcı doğrulaması bu oturumda yapılamadı — dürüstlük payı olarak not ediyorum.

### Faz 12-C-hazırlık (PM'in 12-C öncesi fark ettiği boşluk, coder'a atandı) — [x] TAMAMLANDI (2026-08-26, coder kendi test/QA'sı)
